# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Universal SSR focus programme.
COLDWAR_SSR_offer_clearing = { if = { limit = { COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_clearing = yes } set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_ever_offered country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.700 } } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_cancel_selection_clearing = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_ever_offered }

COLDWAR_SSR_preview_offer_clearing = { effect_tooltip = {
        custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_origin_scope_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_preview_setup_origin = 0.002 tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_setup_origin_share_tt }
        add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_preview_weekly_origin = 0.00003 tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_origin_share_tt }
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_prospective_partner_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_recipient
        add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_preview_setup_recipient = 0.001 tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_setup_recipient_share_tt }
        add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_preview_weekly_recipient = 0.00002 tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_recipient_share_tt }
    } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_dispatch_clearing = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = UKR COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_clearing = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = {
            save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_clearing_UKR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_clearing_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        UKR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6001 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_clearing_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
        UKR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_clearing_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active }
        UKR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_origin_clearing_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active }
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
        remove_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_clearing_BLR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    BLR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_BLR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_clearing_BLR_to = yes
            BLR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            BLR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        BLR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        BLR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6001 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_clearing_BLR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_BLR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_pending
        BLR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_BLR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_BLR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_clearing_BLR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_BLR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_active }
        BLR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_BLR_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_BLR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_BLR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_origin_clearing_BLR = {

        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_MLV_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_MLV

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_clearing_EST = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    EST = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_EST_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_EST
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_clearing_EST_to = yes
            EST = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            EST = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        EST = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_EST_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_EST_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        EST = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6001 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_clearing_EST = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_EST has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_EST_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_EST_pending
        EST = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_EST
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_EST
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_TAJ_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_TAJ_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        TAJ = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6001 } }
}

    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_TAJ has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_TAJ_pending }

        TAJ = {

        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_TAJ

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_technical_UKR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.004 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_technical_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending }
            num_of_factories > 4
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        UKR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_technical_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6002 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_technical_AZE = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    AZE = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.004 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_AZE_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_AZE
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_technical_AZE_to = yes
            AZE = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending }
            num_of_factories > 4
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            AZE = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        AZE = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_technical_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_AZE_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_AZE_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        AZE = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6002 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_technical_TAJ = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    TAJ = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.004 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_TAJ_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_TAJ
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_technical_TAJ_to = yes
            TAJ = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending }
            num_of_factories > 4
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            TAJ = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        TAJ = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_technical_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_TAJ_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_TAJ_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        TAJ = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6002 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_technical_TAJ = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_TAJ has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_TAJ_pending }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_border_MLV = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    MLV = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_MLV_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_border_MLV
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_border_MLV_to = yes
            MLV = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            MLV = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        MLV = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_border_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_MLV_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_MLV_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        MLV = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6003 } }
}

            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_border_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_KYR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_KYR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        KYR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6003 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_border_KYR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_border_KYR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_KYR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_KYR_pending
        KYR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_border_KYR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_border_KYR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_border_TAJ = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    TAJ = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_TAJ_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_border_TAJ
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_border_TAJ_to = yes
            TAJ = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            TAJ = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        TAJ = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_border_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_border_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_TAJ_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_border_from_TAJ_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        TAJ = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6003 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_training_UKR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_training_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending }
            num_divisions > 5
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        UKR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_training_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6004 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_training_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_training_ARM = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    ARM = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_ARM_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_ARM
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_training_ARM_to = yes
            ARM = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending }
            num_divisions > 5
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            ARM = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        ARM = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_training_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_ARM_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_ARM_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        ARM = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6004 } }
}

    }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_training_KYR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    KYR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_KYR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_KYR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_training_KYR_to = yes
            KYR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending }
            num_divisions > 5
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            KYR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        KYR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_training_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_KYR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_KYR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        KYR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6004 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_training_KYR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_KYR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_KYR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_KYR_pending
        KYR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_KYR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_training_KYR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_energy_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_energy_BLR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0025 } }
    BLR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_BLR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_BLR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_energy_BLR_to = yes
            BLR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            BLR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        BLR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_energy_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_BLR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_BLR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        BLR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6005 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_energy_BLR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_BLR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_BLR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_BLR_pending
        BLR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_BLR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_energy_BLR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        TRK = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6005 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_energy_TRK = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_TRK has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_TRK_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_TRK_pending
        TRK = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_TRK
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_energy_TRK
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_energy_KYR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0025 } }
    KYR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_KYR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_KYR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_energy_KYR_to = yes
            KYR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            KYR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        KYR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_energy_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_KYR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_KYR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        KYR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6005 } }
}

        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_KYR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_KYR_pending }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_water_GEO = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } }
    GEO = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_GEO_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_GEO
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_water_GEO_to = yes
            GEO = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            GEO = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        GEO = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_water_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_GEO_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_GEO_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        GEO = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6006 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_water_GEO = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_GEO has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_GEO_pending }

        GEO = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending

        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_water_GEO

        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_UZB
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_water_UZB
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_water_TRK = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } }
    TRK = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_TRK
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_water_TRK_to = yes
            TRK = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            TRK = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        TRK = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_water_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        TRK = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6006 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_water_TRK = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_TRK has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_pending
        TRK = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_TRK
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_water_TRK
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_TRK_active

        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_water_TRK

            country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { tag = KYR COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_romanian = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_KYR
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = {
            save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_KYR
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_KYR_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
            country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { tag = TAJ COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_romanian = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_TAJ
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = {
            save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_TAJ
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TAJ_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
            country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 }
        }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_romanian_UKR = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_romanian_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        UKR = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_romanian_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_romanian_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
        UKR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
            set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_retry_wait value = 1 days = 180 }
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_romanian_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_active }
        UKR = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_active
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
            COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_romanian_LIT = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    LIT = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_LIT
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_romanian_LIT_to = yes
            LIT = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            LIT = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        LIT = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_romanian_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        LIT = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
    }
    else = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 } }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_romanian_LIT = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_LIT has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_pending }

        LIT = {
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_romanian_TRK = {
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    TRK = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TRK_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_TRK
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_romanian_TRK_to = yes
            TRK = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            TRK = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        TRK = {
            set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
            clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
            set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_active
            add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_romanian_origin
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TRK_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TRK_active
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        TRK = { COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes }
